This year, we continue to make important technological investments to provide the most advanced services at our customs doors, where about 25 per cent of our country's tax revenues are collected. By reducing paperwork and increasing automation in customs transactions, we contribute to facilitating trade. In January-November 2021, the number of export declarations in our customs administrations increased by 16.6 per cent compared to the same period of the previous year to 4,290,000. On the other hand, in the first 11 months of the year, some 7 million 250,000 vehicles and 68.5 million passengers were processed in our customs. We continue to strengthen the physical infrastructure and technical capacity of our customs so that we can respond to increasing passenger and vehicle traffic day after day without interruption. In this framework, renovations at Habur and Ipsala Customs Gates were completed in 2021, and the modernisation of the Turkish Eye Customs Gate was launched. Project work on renovating the Ceylanpınar Customs Gate is under way and is aimed at the launch of modernisation of Gurbulak and Marketkule Customs Gates by the end of the year. In addition, the Erzurum Customs Directorate service building will be launched within 2022. Finally, the Kayseri Free Zone Customs Directorate will be operational in 2021, and the number of customs managers and customs managers we will open in Nizip and Havsa. Dear lawmakers, in 2021, we took steps to strengthen our digital infrastructure to make our customs practices, such as the ClGE System, more user-friendly. With the application, all shareholders will be able to make a statement through a single electronic access point, and the declaration process will be significantly shortened. In addition, air transport has been included in pre-existing customs. As part of our joint transit regime with the EU, we're raising the transports under the authorized sender, which is a quick completion of customs, from 1.1% to 10 per cent, so that we can reduce the buildup in these customs. Plus, we're aiming for the export-oriented send-off system, which we're going to bring in at the beginning of next year, to deliver the export load to target markets with a faster and quality logistics presentation. On the other hand, we're making sure that the Easy Export Points we've activated and the customs process of the property is completed at one point. The e-meal system, which has close to 1.2 million members, made 11,000 tenders as of December 1st, and received $664m in revenues compared to the same period of last year, up 67 per cent. Dear lawmakers, we continue our work in the national and international area, especially at our customs doors to fight trafficking. We are constantly investing in our technical and human capital in the fight against trafficking to strengthen our capacity, and we continue to use the newest and most advanced technologies in our customs. As we facilitated the legal trade, we quickly increased the number of vehicles and container scanning systems in our inventory to 74. We aim to build x-ray scanning devices, which are one of the most important detection technologies in the world in the fight against trafficking, with local and national possibilities, and we aim to complete it in the coming period. In early 2022, more than 70 per cent of localization will be provided with our first localized x-ray devices in customs fields. On the other hand, we aim to increase the number of high-resolution and three-dimensional vehicle and container scanning systems in the world's number of customs administrations at the Ipsala Customs Gate for the first time. With our scanning network project, we're setting up a system that can quickly analyze X-ray images of vehicles and containers scanned at customs doors. Thanks to data management and targeting software developed by TUBITAK, we will make customs controls more accurate, faster and more efficient. With all of our work, we will improve the legal trade environment, and we will also make it easier to deal more effectively with organised smuggling initiatives.
